function getQueryString() {







        var querystring = new Array;







        var q = String(document.location).split("?")[1];







        if (!q) {







            return false







        }







        q = q.split("&");







        for (var i = 0; i < q.length; i++) {







            var o = q[i].split("=");







            querystring[o[0]] = o[1]







        }







        return querystring







    }















    function getHashString() {







        var qs = new Array;







        var q = String(document.location).split("#")[1];







        if (!q) {







            return false







        }







        q = q.split("&");







        for (var i = 0; i < q.length; i++) {







            var o = q[i].split("=");







            qs[o[0]] = o[1]







        }







        return qs







    }







</script>

<script>
    /*************************************************************************
     ** Video Lazy Load (can move to global config for all brands if needed)
     ***************************************************************************/
    (function() {
        var videoLazyLoadDetect = function() {
            try {
                var $videoLazyLoad = $('video.lazyload-video');
                if ($videoLazyLoad.length) {
                    $videoLazyLoad.each(function() {
                        var $vid = $(this);
                        if ($vid.is(':visible')) {
                            var $videoSourceElem = $vid.find('source');
                            var videoMainSrc = $vid.filter('data-src').length || '';
                            var src = (videoMainSrc) ? $vid.attr('data-src') : $videoSourceElem.attr(
                                'data-src');

                            if (src) {
                                var $vidContent = $vid[0];
                                $vidContent.pause();
                                $vid.removeClass('lazyload').addClass('lazyloaded-video');
                                (videoMainSrc) ? $vid.attr('src', src): $videoSourceElem.attr('src',
                                    src);
                                $vidContent.load();
                                $vidContent.play();;
                            }
                        }
                    })
                } else {
                    jQuery(window).off('resize scroll', videoLazyLoadDetect);
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.log('LAZYLOAD ERROR: ' + err);
            }
        }
        jQuery(window).on('resize scroll', videoLazyLoadDetect).resize();
    })();

</script>
